- **Capsule Forge**: Use when creating, building, scaffolding, or authoring an Unicity AOS capsule — a sandboxed WASM tool provider that exposes tools to the LLM over a message bus. Everything needed to ship a capsule from zero. ([unicity-aos/oracles](https://github.com/unicity-aos/oracles/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/skills/forge) · ★2 · 2 copies)
